Promote selected findings to [PAPER_OUTLINE.md](PAPER_OUTLINE.md) only when drafting the paper — do not duplicate the full log there. Citations: [PAPER_LEDGER.md](PAPER_LEDGER.md) · [paper.bib](paper.bib).

- **NEW ALL-TIME DATASET A & RAW-AUDIO SINGLE-CHECKPOINT CHAMPION (`EXP-20261128-01`) — Subdivision-selective soft-Dice (`dice_slot_scope="fine_subdiv"`, `16th_off` + `24th` only, zero-gated on sparse charts) SHATTERS `0.790` on Dataset A AND `0.820` on raw audio from ONE checkpoint (`Arm K`: `0.79043` F1@0.50 exact / `0.81303` oracle `evF1` / `7,549` err / `0.8204` raw-audio `evF1` at `100.5%` exact density!):** Restricting soft-Dice strictly to `16th_off` `(12, 36)` + `24th` `(8, 16, 32, 40)` with Laplace `smooth=1.0` and `torch.where(target_sum > 0)` zero-gating eliminated the global-Dice vs no-Dice tradeoff completely — **3/3 arms beat prior `Soup_F_H_I` (`0.78726`) AND prior 3-model logit ensemble (`0.80918` `evF1`)**:
  - **Arm K (`fine_subdiv 0.03, pw=0.92, s=42`, single `.pt`):** **`0.79043` F1@0.50 exact** (`+0.47 pp` vs Arm C, `+8.00 pp` over log-mel ITGPT `0.7104`), **`0.81303` ±20 ms `evF1`**, **all-time lowest `7,549` errors** (`−397` vs Arm C, `−731` fewer FPs, `r=1.016`), **all-time `last.pt 0.78548`**, **all-time transfer grounding `shuffle −0.1466 / zeros −0.1593`** (`ev −0.1583 / −0.1636`), winning EVERY slice (`quarter 0.8575`, `8th 0.7964`, `16th_off 0.6706`, **`24th 0.3889` `+13.16 pp` vs `Soup_F_H_I`!**, **`<=8 0.7402`** `−336` err vs Arm C, `arrow_arrangements 0.8556`, `beast_beats 0.8420`, `tsunamix_iii 0.7390`), and setting a **NEW ALL-TIME RAW-AUDIO RECORD: `0.8204` @ 20 ms (`101.0%` of oracle `0.8125`) / `0.8244` @ 50 ms at `P=0.818 R=0.822` (`100.5%` exact density!)**.
  - **Cross-seed & single-var confirmation:** Arm J (`fine_subdiv 0.02, pw=1.00, s=42`): **`0.78884` / `0.81150` `evF1` / `7,658` err** (`+0.262 pp` over matched `dice=0.0` Arm F `0.78622`); Arm L (`fine_subdiv 0.03, pw=0.92, s=123`): **`0.78721` (`trainer 0.78734`) / `0.80964` `evF1`** (`+0.20 pp` F1 / `+0.35 pp` `evF1` over matched `s=123` Arm I `0.78523`; 2-seed mean **`0.78882 / 0.81134`** vs `0.78573 / 0.80707`); `Soup_K_L` hits `0.78910` (`16th_off 0.6751`), while single **Arm K** wins outright without souping.
- **Prior Slice 3 milestone (`EXP-20261127-11`) — Dropping per-chart soft-Dice (`dice=0.0`) + `keep0 SpecAug` + `rdrop=2.0` + `pw=0.92` + zero-overhead 3-checkpoint Model Soup (`Soup_F_H_I`): `0.78726` F1@0.5 @ `0.50` exact / `0.80846` ±20 ms `evF1` / `7,708` errors (`−238` vs Arm C, `−546` fewer FPs; `+7.69 pp` over log-mel ITGPT!):** Diagnosing Arm C's `+15.2%` low-meter (`<=8`) over-prediction traced it to per-chart soft-Dice giving sparse charts ~9× stronger positive gradients. - **NEW ALL-TIME CHAMPIONS ON BOTH BENCHMARKS — 3-way loss + aux synergy (`beat_aux_head` + `dice_loss_weight=0.05` + `bce_pos_weight`, `EXP-20261127-01`) & R-Drop (`rdrop_kl_weight=2.0`, `EXP-20261127-02`):** A 7-hour autonomous 3-GPU `/swarm` campaign (35 runs across Colab T4 High-RAM, Colab T4, and local WSL RTX 2080) discovered a non-trivial 3-way synergy combining per-beat activity/count auxiliary supervision (`ItgptBeatAuxHead`), masked soft-Dice (`dice_loss_weight=0.05`), and positive class weighting (`bce_pos_weight`), plus bidirectional Bernoulli R-Drop (`rdrop_kl_weight=2.0`, zero extra params / zero inference cost):
  - **NEW ALL-TIME `300t/50v` CHAMPION (`EXP-20261127-01`, `keep0 + beat_aux=0.15 + dice=0.05 + pw=1.25`, `best_slot_f1.pt` ep 20):** **`0.84974` F1@0.5** (`+0.21 pp` vs `d625680` `0.84764`), **FIRST `0.85000` `F1_max`** (@ `0.45`), **`0.84987` ±20 ms `evF1`**, **all-time lowest `11,593` errors** (`−25` vs `11,618`), **`best.pt 0.84783`**, **`last.pt 0.83815`** (`+0.08 pp`), **`24th 0.5358`** (`+2.25 pp` vs `dice005`), **`8th 0.8869`**, `16th_off 0.7278`, and clean grounding (**`shuffle −0.1226`**, **`zeros −0.1018`** — cleanly past both `0.08`/`0.10` guardrails).   - **Negative Tier-C ledger (`EXP-20261127-03`):** 14 extra-parameter architectural/regularization hypotheses were cleanly falsified and kept off `master` (`use_rope_level6`, `subbeat_skip_head`, `slot_refine_head`, `level6_drop_path`, `multiscale_conv_head`, `tversky_alpha=0.6`, `mert_temporal_delta`, `mert_delta_gate`, `chart_density_loss`, `subdiv_aux_head` — won 2/2 Dataset A seeds `0.77661` / `24th 0.3543` but regressed on `300t/50v` `0.84630`, `mask_padded_beats`, `use_swiglu`, `mert_noise_std=0.08`, `decoupled_weight_decay=0.03 + lr=1.5e-4`, `Cosine-32`).
- **Previous `300t/50v` base (`d625680`) — `density + SpecAugment (4/0.15) + preserve_downbeat=true` (`EXP-20261126-15/16/17` — `0.84764–0.84780` F1@0.5, `11,618` errors, `last 0.83732`, `shuffle −0.1255`):** Combining `difficulty_source="density"` with MERT-only SpecAugment (`4/0.15`, `EXP-20261126-15`) delivered a massive super-additive leap (`0.84071 → 0.84780` F1@0.5 / `0.84820` max / `11,655` errors, beating pure `density` on **14/14 slices**, `16th_off 0.7244` `+3.62 pp` vs `#METER`, `24th 0.5311` `+4.05 pp`, `vocaloid 0.8325` `+1.90 pp`, `last 0.83291`). Adding downbeat-preserving masking (`mert_time_mask_preserve_downbeat=true` — never masking beat frame `0`, `EXP-20261126-16`) set **5 new all-time `300t/50v` records** while virtually tying peak F1 (`0.84764` / `0.84808` max / `0.84780` `evF1`): **lowest errors (`11,618`, `−37` vs unpreserved, `−658` vs `0.83883`)**, **highest `best.pt` (`0.84558`, `val_loss 0.06689`)**, **highest `last.pt` (`0.83732`, `+0.44 pp` vs unpreserved, `+0.14 pp` vs Keras L6 `0.8359`, `+1.64 pp` vs PyTorch base)**, **strongest `shuffle` grounding (`−0.1255`)**, and **`zeros −0.1049`** (`+1.95 pp` recovery over pure `density`), with `meter 13+ 0.8746`, `itl 0.8570`, `mizuki 0.8504`, `8th 0.8861`, `intro 0.8015`. Adding an explicit 3rd `NPS` scalar condition token (`use_nps_condition=true`, `EXP-20261126-17`) was a wash (`0.84731` / `11,715` err / `last 0.83433`) — confirming `level6` already captures `density × bpm` and proving the **`0.8473–0.8478` (`+0.85–0.90 pp`) jump is rock-solid across 3 independent runs**. Keep `use_nps_condition=false`.
- **NEW ALL-TIME DATASET A CHAMPION — `density` (`ls=0.01`, `EXP-20261126-15` — `0.77286` F1@0.5 / `0.77397` max / `0.79228` ±20 ms `evF1` / `8,166` errors; beats BOTH PyTorch `0.7691` AND Keras `0.7711`):** Applying `difficulty_source="density"` to Dataset A (`L2+6+10+16 + Cosine-64 + EMA + ls=0.01`) pushed `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 20) to a new all-time single-checkpoint Dataset A record across every headline metric: **`0.77286`** F1@0.5 (`+0.38 pp` vs PyTorch `#METER` `0.7691`, `+0.18 pp` vs Keras `0.7711`), **`0.77397`** max @ `0.45`, **`0.79228`** ±20 ms `evF1` (first single checkpoint past `0.79`!), **`8,166` errors** (lowest ever), `ratio 1.012`, **`shuffle −0.1447`**, **`zeros −0.4436`** (`3.6×` stronger than `#METER` `ls=0.01`), `8th 0.7704` (`+1.69 pp`), `16th_off 0.6353` (`+2.44 pp`), **`24th 0.3329` (`+14.97 pp`)**, `13+ 0.8549` (`+1.32 pp`), and lifted zero-shot `Dataset A → 300t/50v` by **`+2.98 pp`** (`0.6674 → 0.69712`, `itl 0.8455`, `vocaloid 0.8095`, `mizuki +4.47 pp`). Adding SpecAugment (`4/0.15`, `EXP-20261126-16`) on 81-song `ls=0.01` Dataset A set new all-time **`24th 0.3776`** & **`shuffle −0.1469`** (`last −0.1478`) records and boosted `last 0.76315` (`+0.72 pp`), but slightly trailed pure `density` on peak F1 (`0.76825` unpreserved / `0.76606` `keep0`, which restored `16th_off +4.27 pp` & `zeros −0.3751`) because `ls=0.01` already regularizes Dataset A.
- **Zero-training decoding strategies (`--diagnose_decoding`, `EXP-20261126-18`):** Evaluated `fixed_05`, `topk_density`, `topk_clamped`, and leakage-free leave-one-song-out `loso_subdiv` / `loso_subdiv_topk` across 4 in-domain + 3 zero-shot settings on BOTH datasets. **Clean rule:** (1) **In-domain** (`ratio ≈ 1.01–1.04`): **`fixed_05` wins every single run** (`300t/50v` `0.8478` vs `0.8454`, Dataset A `0.7729` vs `0.7586`) because the density-conditioned model is already well-calibrated; (2) **Zero-shot with density mismatch**: **`topk_density`** delivers a free **`+0.40–0.57 pp` F1 / `+0.91–0.95 pp` `evF1` / `−1,055` to `−1,086` fewer errors** on `300t/50v → Dataset A` (`keep0` `0.73286 → 0.73853`, `evF1 0.75613 → 0.76522`, `10,399 → 9,344` err), while **`loso_subdiv_topk`** jumps **`+0.88 pp` F1 (`0.69712 → 0.70588`) / `+1.47 pp` `evF1` (`0.69715 → 0.71186`)** on `Dataset A → 300t/50v` (`mizuki +2.54 pp`, `9–10 +3.52 pp`, `topk_clamped` `0.69803` / `evF1 0.70316` with `16th +4.38 pp` & `24th +4.80 pp`).
- **Pre-scaling round-2 ablations closed (`EXP-20261126-12/13/14`):** Pure `density` (`EXP-20261126-14`: `0.84071`) broke the `0.8388` `#METER` ceiling and beat in-domain Fraxtil MERT zero-shot on `13+`/`16th`/`24th`. Matched 128-ep **`DDCL-MERT`** (`L2+6+10+16 + Cosine-128 + EMA`, `EXP-20261126-12`) closed the 64-ep gap (`0.7368 → 0.74908` @ `0.50`, `evF1 0.76365`, `zeros −0.7476` `99.6%` suppression), landing `−0.20 pp` behind `L6 const128` (`0.7511`) and leaving **ITGPT-MERT (`0.7729` density / `0.7691` `#METER`) `+2.18 / +1.80 pp` ahead** at matched convergence. Lighter MERT-only SpecAugment (`2 / 0.05`, `EXP-20261126-13`) was **REJECTED on both datasets** (`300t/50v` `0.83603`, Dataset A `0.76389`).
- **PyTorch Dataset A `label_smoothing = 0.01` + per-block `torch.compile(dynamic=True)` (`EXP-20261126-06` — `#METER` PyTorch record `0.7691` / `0.7719` max / `0.7848` `evF1` at `13.5 s/ep`, 3.5× faster than Keras; superseded by `EXP-20261126-15` `density` `0.77286`):** Running [`itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001.json) on Colab `L4` (`bf16` + TF32 + per-block `module.compile(dynamic=True)`) lifted `best.pt` (ep 17, `val_loss 0.06091`, fp32 eval) to **0.7691** F1@0.5 (`+0.41 pp` vs PyTorch `ls=0.0` `0.7650`, within `0.20 pp` of Keras `ls=0.01` `0.7711`), **0.7719** `F1_max` @ `0.45` (within `0.03 pp` of Keras `0.7722`), **0.7848** ±20 ms `micro_event_f1` (`+0.17 pp` vs PyTorch `ls=0.0`, `+0.34 pp` vs Keras `ls=0.0`), **99.1%** density calibration (`17,713/17,868`, `8,213` total errors), and healthy grounding (`shuffle −0.1390` / `event −0.1460`, `zeros −0.1220`). `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 16): `0.7687` / `0.7700` max; `last.pt` (ep 64): `0.7508` (same late drift as Keras `ls=0.01` `0.7556`). Per-block `torch.compile` cut Ep 1 compile **4.5×** (`491 s → 109 s` train / `146 s` Ep 1 total) and sped steady epochs to **`13.5 s/ep`** on `L4` (`17.3 s/ep` on `T4` `fp16` smoke with `cache_size_limit=16`, zero recompiles). Direct Drive → Colab streaming unpacked `__meta.tar` (`11.2 MiB` in `1.7 s`) + `__mert_l2_6_10_16.tar` (`4.7 GiB` in `50.2 s @ 94.3 MiB/s`).
- **PyTorch Dataset A parity verified (`pt_v2` post-fix, Colab `L4` `bf16`, `EXP-20261126-05` — NEW PY-TORCH PARITY RECORD: `best.pt` 0.7831 ±20 ms `micro_event_f1` BEATS Keras `0.7814`, `0.7693` max-F1 matches Keras `0.7698`, `0.7650` F1@0.5, `zeros −0.5272` 2.3× stronger, `16.2 s/ep` 2.9× faster):** Re-running `L2+6+10+16 + Cosine-64 + EMA` on Colab `L4` after fixing deterministic per-epoch validation produced a razor-smooth `val_loss` curve (`0.1167 → 0.04564` @ ep 18), lifting `best.pt` (ep 18, fp32 eval) to **0.7650** F1@0.5 (`+0.32 pp` vs pre-fix `best.pt` `0.7618`), **0.7693** `F1_max` @ `0.40` (within `0.05 pp` of Keras `0.7698`), **0.7831** ±20 ms `micro_event_f1` (**+0.17 pp** over Keras `0.7814`), **99.8%** exact density (`17,825` vs `17,868`), and **−0.5272** `zeros` silence suppression (`2.3×` stronger than Keras `−0.2308`, `1 − IoU 0.824`). `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 32): **0.7645** F1@0.5 / **0.7819** `micro_event_f1` / **`shuffle −0.1443`** (`+0.47 pp` stronger than Keras). `last.pt` (ep 64): `0.7524`. Full PyTorch parity is confirmed at **2.9× L4 throughput** (`16.2 s/ep` vs `47 s/ep`).
- **PyTorch Dataset A parity (`L2+6+10+16 + Cosine-64 + EMA`, Colab `L4` `bf16`, `EXP-20261126-04` — pre-fix run, deterministic-val bug found & fixed):** First PyTorch Colab L4 parity run against Keras `EXP-20261125-10` (`0.7680` / `0.7698` max / `0.7814` event F1) hit `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 24, fp32) **0.7623** F1@0.5 (`F1_max 0.7675` @ `0.40`, `8,454` errors), **0.7814** ±20 ms `micro_event_f1` (exact match), and stronger audio grounding (`shuffle −0.1407`, `zeros −0.2408`), running **2.8× faster** (`17.3 s/ep` `bf16` vs Keras `47 s/ep` `fp16`). Audit against pre-cutover Keras (`946cc8f`) identified a **real trainer bug**: `val_iter` / `val_rng` was never reset per epoch in PyTorch (`itgpt`, `ddcl`, `ddc`), causing `val_loss` to swing `±0.020` across random 20-chart draws and `best.pt` to misfire at ep 18 (`0.03216` → `0.7618`). Fixed per-epoch deterministic validation across all three trainers (`3d32edb`); verified in `EXP-20261126-05`.
- **Dataset A Knob 8 (Batch 2 #4, Colab parallel `L4` + `T4`) — Mild binary label smoothing (`label_smoothing = 0.01` & `0.02`, `EXP-20261126-03` — NEW PEAK/EVENT/GROUNDING RECORD: `ls=0.01` `best.keras` 0.7711 F1@0.5 / 0.7722 max-F1 / +0.6904 ±20 ms skill, with late `last` drift to 0.7556):** Adding default-off binary label smoothing (`y → (1−ε)y + 0.5ε`) inside `ItgptPlacementLoss` on top of `L2+6+10+16 + Cosine-64 + EMA` (`0.7680`) pushed `ls=0.01` (`best.keras` ep 26, fp32) to new Dataset A single-checkpoint highs on **`slot_f1_at_05` (0.7711, `+0.31 pp`)**, **`slot_f1_max` (0.7722 @ `0.45`, `+0.24 pp`)**, **±20 ms `micro_event_f1` (0.7873, `+0.59 pp`)**, and **`skill_event_f1` (+0.6904, `+0.91 pp`)**, with stronger audio grounding (`shuffle −0.1427`, `zeros −0.7357` → `0.0` skill on silence) and balanced density (`+1.7%`, `8,250` errors). `ls=0.02` (`best.keras` ep 17, fp32) matched `slot_f1_at_05` (**0.7711 @ `0.50`** exact calibration) with slightly lower `F1_max` (`0.7711`), `micro_event_f1` (`0.7860`), and `shuffle` drop (`−0.1353`). Caveat: both smoothed runs overfit later in the 64-ep schedule (`last` `0.7556` / `0.7574` vs unsmoothed `0.7650`), so **`label_smoothing = 0.01` is a top early-stopped candidate** to test alongside unsmoothed `0.0` on `300t/50v`.
- **Dataset A Knob 7 (Batch 2 #3) — Random bar-aligned beat-shift augmentation (`max_bar_shift = 15`, `EXP-20261126-02` — REJECTED, `−1.42 pp` vs `L2+6+10+16 + EMA`):** Randomly prepending `0..60` masked beats (`4k`) during training regressed `best_slot_f1` (ep 40, fp32) from **0.7680 → 0.7538** (`−1.42 pp`; `last 0.7465`, `−1.85 pp`; `skill +0.6638`, `−1.75 pp`; errors `8,182 → 8,540`), driven by a **−13.7 pp collapse on `intro (0–10%)`** (`0.622 → 0.485`) because random shifts broke `level3/4/5` phrase-chunk and `level6_pos(0..32)` beat-0 alignment between train and eval. **Keep `max_bar_shift = 0`.**
- **Dataset A Knob 6 (Batch 2 #2) — Hybrid MERT + 3-band spectral flux (`mert_spectral_flux = true`, `EXP-20261126-01` — REJECTED, `−0.78 pp` vs `L2+6+10+16 + EMA`):** Appending 6 z-scored 100 Hz low/mid/high spectral-flux & energy channels from `*.ddc_mel.npy` onto MERT `layers 2,6,10,16` regressed `best_slot_f1` (ep 40, fp32) from **0.7680 → 0.7602** (`−0.78 pp`; `last 0.7532`, `−1.18 pp`; `val_loss 0.04842` vs `0.04661`; `skill +0.6736`; errors `8,182 → 8,394`) and cut `zeros` grounding in half (`−0.2308 → −0.1076`). Slight `16th` uptick (`0.600 → 0.621`) was overwhelmed by cross-song raw-STFT overfitting on `quarter`, `intro`, and low meters. **Keep `mert_spectral_flux = false`** (pure MERT `2,6,10,16`, no `*.ddc_mel.npy` needed for `final_data`).
- **Dataset A Knob 5 (Batch 2 #1) — Static 2D MERT `layers 2,6,10,16` + Cosine-64 + EMA (`EXP-20261125-10` — PROMOTED, NEW ALL-TIME SINGLE-CHECKPOINT DATASET A CHAMPION: 0.7680 F1@0.5 / 0.7709 `last` max-F1 / 8,182 errors):** Dropping `L0` and pre-averaging Run 2's top 4 layers `(2, 6, 10, 16)` into a standard 2D `(T, 1024)` cache (1× RAM, 1× speed, 0 extra params) pushed `best.keras` (ep 41, fp32) to **0.7680** F1@0.5 (`P=0.7784 R=0.7579`, **8,182** total errors — lowest ever, `−172` fewer than `L2+6+10 + EMA`), `F1_max` **0.7698** @ `0.45`, `zeros` drop **−0.2308** (`+8.1 pp` stronger silence suppression), `shuffle −0.1396`, and lifted `last.keras` (ep 64) to a new all-time record **0.7650** F1@0.5 / **0.7709** max-F1 (peaking at **0.7723** max-F1 @ ep 48 — first single checkpoint past `0.770` max-F1). New pack highs on `fraxtil_s_arrow_arrangements` (**0.8427**) and `tsunamix_iii` (**0.7180**), `<=1 slot` micro-jitter down `6.9%/7.4% → 6.0%/5.3%`. **PROMOTED as the MERT extraction recipe.**
- **Dataset A Knob 4 — `AdamW` EMA (`use_ema = true`, `ema_momentum = 0.995`, `SwapEMAWeights`, `EXP-20261125-09` — PROMOTED, NEW ALL-TIME SINGLE-CHECKPOINT DATASET A CHAMPION: 0.7679 F1@0.5 / +0.6871 ±20 ms skill):** Enabling built-in Keras 3 EMA weight averaging + `SwapEMAWeights(swap_on_epoch=True)` on `L2+6+10 + Cosine-64` lifted `best_slot_f1.keras` (ep 40, fp32) to **0.7679** F1@0.5 (**+0.15 pp** vs `L2+6+10` `best` `0.7664`, **+0.34 pp** vs `best_slot_f1` `0.7645`, **+5.75 pp** vs log-mel ITGPT `0.7104`), **+0.6871** decoded ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` (**+0.34 pp**, new single-ckpt record; `event_f1 0.7848`), `shuffle −0.1419` (**7.8×** mel DDCL), and stabilized late convergence (`last` **0.7638**, **+0.40 pp** vs `0.7599`, staying `>=0.7636` across ep 40–64) with zero extra inference cost or RAM. Lifted `intro` **+3.6 pp** (`0.626 → 0.663`), `8th` **+2.2 pp** (`0.740 → 0.762`), `fraxtil_s_arrow_arrangements` **0.8413**, and `tsunamix_iii` **0.7166** (note: smoothed rare `24th` triplet spikes on the 81-song set). **PROMOTED alongside Cosine-64 and multi-layer MERT.**
- **Dataset A Knob 3 — `gradient_accumulation_steps = 4` (`EXP-20261125-08` — REJECTED at matched 64-ep `lr=1e-4`, `−1.73 pp`):** Accumulating 4 charts per AdamW update (`3,200` vs `12,800` updates at fixed `lr=1e-4`, 40% faster at `241 ms/step`) hit a low `val_loss` (**0.04528**) and strong silence suppression (`zeros −0.5405`), but under-trained on recall (`best_slot_f1`/`last` ep 64, fp32: **0.7491** F1@0.5, `P=0.785 R=0.716`, max-F1 **0.7610** @ `0.30`, `skill_event_f1` `+0.6490`, `16th 0.587`, `24th 0.090`, still climbing at ep 64). **Keep `gradient_accumulation_steps = 1`** at the 64-ep `lr=1e-4` budget.
- **Dataset A Knob 2 — Learnable 5-layer MERT mixer (`layers 0,2,6,10,16`, `EXP-20261125-07` — MIXED WIN / STRONG INSIGHT: `best_slot_f1 0.7661`, `skill +0.6847` NEW RECORD, `last 0.7633` +0.35 pp, `16th 0.680` +4.8 pp, `shuffle −0.1457` NEW RECORD):** Adding SUPERB-style learnable softmax weights (`5` params) over stacked fp16 MERT layers `(0, 2, 6, 10, 16)` matched peak F1@0.5 (`0.7661` vs `L2+6+10` `0.7664`), set new Dataset A records on `val_loss` (**0.04510** vs `0.04550`), `F1_max` (**0.7699**), decoded ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` (**+0.6847**), `last` F1 (**0.7633**, `+0.35 pp` / max-F1 **0.7689**, `+0.43 pp`), `16th` F1 (**0.680**, `+4.8 pp`!), and audio grounding (`shuffle −0.1457`, **8.0×** mel DDCL). Learned softmax weights cleanly ranked **`L10 (0.223) > L6 (0.208) > L16 (0.204) > L2 (0.186) > L0 (0.180)`** — validating multi-layer blending centered on `L6–L10` + `L16` while confirming a pre-averaged 2D cache captures nearly all peak F1 at 1× RAM/speed.
- **Dataset A Knob 1 — Continuous cross-beat `Conv1D` (`continuous_beat_conv = true`, `EXP-20261125-06` — REJECTED, `−1.17 pp` vs `L2+6+10`):** Running `ItgptBeatMertEncoder`'s `Conv1D(k=3)` continuously over `(B, T*24, D)` instead of per-beat `(B*T, 24, D)` regressed `best.keras` (ep 40, fp32) from **0.7664 → 0.7547** F1@0.5 (`−1.17 pp`; max-F1 **0.7612** @ `0.35`, `−0.85 pp`; ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` `+0.6654`, `−1.83 pp`; `last` **0.7490**, `val_loss 0.04603` vs `0.04550`), hurting every subdivision (`quarter 0.839`, `8th 0.727`, `16th 0.619`, `24th 0.220`; errors `8,288 → 8,650`). Per-beat zero-padding acts as an implicit downbeat-boundary cue before `level1` positional embeddings. **Keep `continuous_beat_conv = false`.**
- **Pre-scaling Run 3 — Cyclic `beat % 4` + `beat % 16` position (`cyclic_measure_pos = true`, `EXP-20261125-05` — REJECTED, `−0.52 pp` vs Cosine-64):** Adding zero-initialized `beat % 4` + `beat % 16` embeddings in `level6` (`5,120` params) scored **0.8336** F1@0.5 (`best_slot_f1` ep 56, fp32; `−0.52 pp` vs Cosine-64 **0.8388**; ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` `+0.7540`, `−0.74 pp`; `zeros` drop weakened `−0.1172 → −0.0932`), nudging quarters/8ths slightly (`+0.2 / +0.1 pp`) at the cost of `16th` (`0.700 → 0.676`) and `24th` (`0.504 → 0.475`). **Keep `cyclic_measure_pos = false`** — ITGPT's hierarchical 4-beat (`level2`) & 16-beat (`level3`) chunks already capture local bar/phrase structure.
- **Pre-scaling Run 2 — `grid_weight_24th = 2.0` on `300t/50v` (`EXP-20261125-04` — REJECTED, `−1.18 pp` vs Cosine-64):** Doubling the 24th/triplet slot BCE weight (`1.0 → 2.0`, matching 16th weight) caused severe overfitting (`loss 0.0242 / val_loss 0.1406` vs `0.0402 / 0.0784` @ ep 64) and regressed `best.keras` (ep 19, fp32) to **0.8270** F1@0.5 (`−1.18 pp` vs Cosine-64 **0.8388**; max-F1 **0.8319** @ `0.40`, `−0.69 pp`; ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` `+0.7472`, `−1.42 pp`; `shuffle` drop weakened to `−0.1090`), hurting both `16th` (`0.700 → 0.628`) and `24th` (`0.504 → 0.469`). **Keep `grid_weight_24th = 1.0`** — acoustic representation (Run 1 multi-layer MERT `5.5×` triplet jump), not loss reweighting, is what unlocks fine subdivisions.
- **Dataset A Multi-Layer MERT (`layers 2,6,10`, `EXP-20261125-03` — NEW ALL-TIME DATASET A CHAMPION: 0.7664 F1@0.5 / 0.7697 max-F1 / +0.6837 ±20 ms skill):** Replacing single layer-6 MERT with a per-layer-normalized mean of layers `(2, 6, 10)` (exact same `1024`-d shape, zero extra params/disk/RAM) on Dataset A Cosine-64 pushes `best.keras` (ep 38, `val_loss 0.04550`, fp32) to **0.7664** F1@0.5 (**+0.84 pp** over layer-6 Cosine-64 **0.7580**, **+1.45 pp** over layer-6 `const128` **0.7519**, **+5.60 pp** over paper-faithful log-mel ITGPT **0.7104**, **+8.60 pp** over log-mel DDCL **0.6804**), **0.7697** max-F1 @ `0.40` (**+0.89 pp**), and **+0.6837** decoded ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` (**+0.85 pp** vs layer-6, **+7.52 pp** vs mel ITGPT). Total errors drop `9,248 → 8,288` (**−10.4%**); `16th` jumps **+3.4 pp** (`0.598 → 0.632`), `24th` jumps **5.5×** (`0.046 → 0.253`, **+20.7 pp**), `intro` **+4.7 pp** (`0.580 → 0.626`), `fraxtil_s_arrow_arrangements` **0.8280** (`+2.2 pp`), `fraxtil_s_beast_beats` **0.8176** (`+1.4 pp`). `last.keras` (ep 64, fp32) hits **0.7599** / **0.7646** max-F1 with **`−0.1437` `shuffle` drop** (**7.9×** mel DDCL — strongest Dataset A audio grounding yet). **PROMOTED as the MERT feature representation for future full `final_data` extraction.**
- **Dataset A Cosine-64 layer-6 (`EXP-20261125-02`):** `best_slot_f1.keras` (ep 24, fp32) **0.7580** F1@0.5 (`+0.61 pp` over `const128` `0.7519`), `skill_event_f1 +0.6752`, `last` **0.7562** (`shuffle −0.1402`). Superseded by `EXP-20261125-03` multi-layer MERT.
- **Ablation 3 — Single-knob Early FiLM (`early_film = true`, `EXP-20261125-01` — REJECTED, `−0.92 pp` vs Cosine-64):** Injecting zero-initialized `[difficulty, bpm]` FiLM before `level1` and `level6` accelerated early convergence (`0.8207` vs `0.8070` @ ep 16) then overfit/stalled (`best_slot_f1` ep 40 fp32 **0.8296** vs **0.8388**, max-F1 **0.8308**, ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` `+0.7489` vs `+0.7614`, `val_loss` `0.0852` vs `0.0784` @ ep 64), hurting `24th` (`0.504 → 0.465`), `16th` (`0.700 → 0.678`), and `meter 13+` (`0.850 → 0.829`) without lifting `meter <= 8` F1 (`0.778 → 0.774`). **Keep `early_film = false`** — ITGPT's late-only conditioning protects difficulty-invariant sub-beat audio representations.
- **Ablation 2 — Single-knob Focal Loss (`focal_gamma = 1.0`, `EXP-20261124-04` — REJECTED, `−0.92 pp` vs Cosine-64):** Adding only `focal_gamma = 1.0` on top of Cosine-64 regressed `best_slot_f1.keras` (ep 56, fp32) from **0.8388 → 0.8296** (`−0.92 pp` F1@0.5; max-F1 **0.8315** @ `0.45`, `−0.73 pp`; ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` `+0.7614 → +0.7504`, `−1.10 pp`), hurting the exact target slices (`16th` `0.700 → 0.669`, `24th` `0.504 → 0.497`, `intro` `0.797 → 0.767`) due to over-weighting irreducible human charting ambiguity. **Keep `focal_gamma = 0.0` (plain grid-weighted BCE); Cosine-64 (`0.8388`) stays champion.**
- **Ablation 1 — Single-knob 64-ep Cosine LR (`EXP-20261124-03` — ACTIVE ALL-TIME `final_data` CHAMPION: 0.8388 F1@0.5 / +0.7614 ±20 ms skill):** Swapping only the LR schedule on `300t/50v` from 128-ep constant `1e-4` to 64-ep warmup (`4` ep) + cosine decay (`1e-4 → 1e-6`) lifts `best_slot_f1.keras` (ep 56, fp32) to **0.8388** F1@0.5 (`0.8366` fp16; **+1.12 pp** over `EXP-20261124-02` `best` **0.8276**), **0.8388** max-F1 @ **`0.50`** (**+0.64 pp** over **0.8324** @ `0.35`), and **+0.7614** decoded ±20 ms `skill_event_f1` (**+1.52 pp** over **+0.7462**). `last.keras` (ep 64) converges cleanly at **0.8359** (**+1.82 pp** over constant-LR `last` **0.8177**). Audio grounding strengthens (`shuffle` drop **−0.1151**, `zeros` drop **−0.1172**), train–val gap narrows to **6.1 pp** (`0.8999` train / `0.8388` val, **74.3%** train→val audio transfer), and subdivision error breakdown shows **+4.8 pp** on 16th off-beats (`0.652 → 0.700`) and **+3.8 pp** on 24th triplets (`0.466 → 0.504`) in **half the epochs** (~2 hr vs ~2.3 hr; 64 ep is now the promoted ablation base).
- **`final_data` 3-point scaling curve (`EXP-20261124-02` — constant-LR 128-ep baseline):** Scaling **ITGPT-MERT** across `48 → 197 → 294` train charts (`277` songs; frozen `48` val charts / `17,057` beats / `37,617` GT steps) progressed `0.7719 → 0.8181 → 0.8276` F1@0.5 (`0.8324` max-F1, `skill_event_f1 +0.7462`, `last` transfer `32.0% → 42.3% → 44.7%`).
- **Dataset A literature 2×2 factorization (`81/9` songs, `45` val charts, `17,523` beats; completed in `EXP-20261123-02/03`, upgraded by `EXP-20261125-02`..`EXP-20261128-01`):** Log-mel DDCL **0.6804** (`shuffle −0.0181`) · Log-mel ITGPT **0.7104** (`−0.1192`, **6.6×** DDCL) · **DDCL-MERT 0.7511** (`−0.1312`, **7.3×**, `zeros 0.000`, **+7.07 pp**) · **ITGPT-MERT `const128` 0.7519 / 0.7633 max-F1** (`−0.1296`, **+4.15 pp**) · **ITGPT-MERT `density` (`EXP-20261126-15`) 0.77286 / 0.79228 `evF1`** · **ITGPT-MERT from-scratch (`EXP-20261127-01`) 0.77971 / 0.80085 `evF1` (`0.77585 ± 0.00341` 3-run mean)** · **Full `final_data` (`1,713t`) ZERO-SHOT (`EXP-20261127-04`) `0.77870` / `0.80337` `evF1`** · **Pretrained `1,713t` → Fine-Tuned `fine_subdiv` Arm K (`EXP-20261128-01`) `0.79043` / `0.81303` `evF1` (`7,549` err, `+8.00 pp` over log-mel ITGPT!)** · **Raw-audio end-to-end (`EXP-20261127-10` / `EXP-20261128-01`, 0 `.sm` beats): `0.8204` Arm K (`101.0%` oracle, `100.5%` density), `0.8196` ZS `1,713t` (`102.2%` oracle), `0.8193` Arm C (`101.4%` oracle)!**
**Next action:** Recommended next `1,713t` foundation run based on `EXP-20261128-03`: keep `d_model=256, n_heads=8, n_enc_layers=6` + `L2+6+10+16` + `Cosine-32` + `EMA` + `density` + `keep0 SpecAug (4/0.15)` + `beat_aux=0.15` + global `dice=0.05` (or `fine_subdiv 0.03`) + `pw=1.15–1.20` + lighter **`silence_negative_prob=0.02`** (100% silence suppression without pushing τ above `0.50`) + `rdrop_kl_weight=1.0` (~1.5 h `L4`); optionally follow with Dataset A fine-tuning (`--init_weights`, `fine_subdiv 0.03`) or paper drafting (`PAPER_OUTLINE.md` + `PAPER_LEDGER.md`). Do not grow B.
**Blockers:** 10 negative-BPM Vocaloid songs excluded cleanly across full `final_data` (`hold_release_rakshasa_and_carcasses`, `news_39`, `pantomime`, `splatter_party`, `the_daughter_of_evil`, `the_rampage_of_hatsune_miku`, `the_servant_of_evil`, `toluthin_antenna`, `viva_happy`, `vrosteroid`). GPU free.
**Defer:** Step selection until asked; 200-ep **declined**; Vol. 4 / larger B (**user declined**); citing B **0.735** vs A **0.710** as one table; citing vs **0.78 / 0.80**; `final_data` as a literature number; AR-on-times locality.

**Recommended when resuming onset work:**

- **First, for any track:** report the audio-blind floor beside every number ([EXP-20260804-03](#exp-20260804-03-every-ladder-rung-is-at-or-below-an-audio-blind-baseline--the-metric-not-the-data-is-what-broke)). A Hungarian F1 on dense charts is unreadable without it.
- **Second, for any track:** run the audio-corruption ablation before believing any score ([EXP-20260804-05](#exp-20260804-05-the-ar-pointer-never-reads-the-audio--the-head-is-absolute-index-classification-not-a-pointer)). A single-song overfit **cannot** detect an audio-blind model — the tide gate passes with the audio reversed.
- **Track B (AR):** the pointer head is the bug, not the data. Replace `Dense(max_patches)` with a content-based pointer against encoder memory before adding rows or tuning anything else.
- **Track A (literature):** Dataset A 2-way `M-slot48`: ITGPT last F1@0.5 **0.710** vs DDCL **0.680** ([EXP-20260819-03](#exp-20260819-03-itgpt-48-slot-placement-128-ep-on-dataset-a), [EXP-20260817-01](#exp-20260817-01-ddcl-48-slot-placement-128-ep-on-dataset-a)). DDC peak-snap **0.364** vs null **0.397** ([EXP-20260819-04](#exp-20260819-04-ddc-hamming-peak-snap-onto-m-slot48-on-dataset-a)); literature DDC stays `M-ddc-20ms` **0.652 / 0.734**. Stay on onset. `final_data` is transfer only — not the comparison set.
- **Event track (optional):** Continue K-query probes on `data/v2` in parallel if not blocking Track A.

### EXP-20261128-03: Pre-`1,713t` 4-arm screen on `300t/50v` + Arm K (`0.79043`) error breakdown audit (`silneg=0.04` eliminates `zeros` FPs `35,462 → 0`; `finedice003 pw=1.15` hits `0.84890`; `d_model=384` hits `intro 0.8200` / `last 0.83974`)

#### 1. Arm K (`EXP-20261128-01`, `0.79043`) local `--error_breakdown` audit

- Evaluated `Arm K` `best_slot_f1.pt` on Dataset A val (`45` charts, `17,523` beats): `OVERALL (tau=0.50)`: **`F1 = 0.7904`** (`P = 0.7842, R = 0.7967`), `TP = 14,236, FP = 3,917, FN = 3,632`, `err = 7,549`.
- **Subdivisions:** `quarter 0.858` (`40.8%` err), `8th 0.796` (`26.5%`), `16th_off 0.671` (`11.2%`), `24th 0.389` (`7.6%`), `32nd 0.000` (`2.8%`), `micro 0.000` (`11.1%`).
- **Timing offsets:** Only `10.3%` of FPs and `11.2%` of FNs are `<=1` slot near-misses (`2–3 slots`: `0.4%` FP / `0.9%` FN; `4–6 slots`: `0.7%` FP / `2.7%` FN; `7–12 slots`: `8.9%` FP / `22.2%` FN; `other same-beat`: `41.4%` FP / `26.3%` FN; `other beat`: `38.3%` FP / `36.7%` FN).
- **Sections & worst charts:** `intro F1 = 0.7382` (`P = 0.687, R = 0.798, FP = 267, FN = 148`) trails `body F1 = 0.7957` (`P = 0.795, R = 0.797`); the bottom 8 charts are all `m=1 beginner` charts: `tsunamix_iii/west (0.212)`, `right_osiris (0.227)`, `kokeshi_neko_medley (0.266)`, `stay_awake (0.286)`, plus `tsunamix_iii/isis (0.270–0.349)`.

#### 2. Infrastructure & regression fixes landed alongside the screen

- `scripts/colab_run_remote.sh` (`4edc539`): fixed `--eval-cmd` execution from `shlex.split(...)` to `bash -lc` so chained `&&` eval commands run remotely instead of passing literal `&&` as an argument to the first Python command.
- `src/stepcovnet/itgpt/{config.py,trainers.py}` (`4edc539`): added default-off `silence_negative_prob: float = 0.0` (zeroes audio + slot targets over a full chart or 4–32 beat span while keeping `difficulty` and `bpm` intact).
- `src/stepcovnet/itgpt/trainers.py` + `tests/itgpt/trainers_test.py` (`c8c5435`, `a927ec8`): extracted `resolve_train_num_workers()` returning `0` when `lazy_mert_cache_size > 0` to prevent 2× DataLoader worker RAM duplication OOM on standard 12.7 GB Colab T4 VMs.

#### 3. `300t/50v` 4-arm screen vs `EXP-20261127-01` (`n_charts=48, n_beats=17,057, n_ref=37,617`)

| ------------- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |
| **Baseline (`EXP-20261127-01`, global `dice 0.05`, `pw=1.25`, `d=256`)** | L4 | **`0.84974`** (ep 20) | **`0.85000` @ 0.45** | **`0.84987`** | **`11,593`** (`32,780 / 6,756 / 4,837`) | **`0.84783`** | `0.83815` | `0.1226` | `0.1018` | `0.9399 / 0.8869 / 0.7278 / 0.5358` | `0.8015 / — / —` |
| **Arm 1 (`finedice 0.03`, `pw=1.00`, `d=256`)** | L4 | `0.84602` (ep 16) | `0.84688` @ 0.45 | `0.84608` | `11,763` (`32,316 / 6,462 / 5,301`, `38,778`) | `0.84445` | `0.83415` | `0.12289` | `0.10532` (`35,462`) | `0.9403 / 0.8871 / 0.7182 / 0.4873` | `0.7979 / 0.8494 / 0.8486` |
| **Arm 2 (`finedice 0.03`, `pw=1.15`, `d=256`, `lazy=64`)** | T4 | `0.84890` (ep 20) | `0.84893` @ 0.45 | `0.84910` | `11,663` (`32,761 / 6,807 / 4,856`, `39,568`) | `0.84536` | `0.83526` | **`0.12470`** | `0.10593` (`39,541`) | `0.9391 /` **`0.8873`** `/ 0.7271 /` **`0.5401`** | `0.8008 /` **`0.8522`** `/` `0.8525` |

#### Takeaways & decision for `1,713t`
- **Global `dice=0.05, pw=1.25, d=256` (`EXP-20261127-01`) retains the `300t/50v` headline record (`0.84974`, `11,593` err).**
- **`fine_subdiv 0.03` needs `pw=1.15` (not `1.00`) on `300t/50v`:** Arm 2 (`pw=1.15`) reached `0.84890` (`−0.08 pp` vs `0.84974`, `+0.29 pp` over Arm 1 `pw=1.00`), setting `24th = 0.5401` (`+0.43 pp`), `8th = 0.8873`, `body = 0.8522`, and `shuffle = −0.12470`, whereas `pw=1.00` under-recalled `24th` (`0.4873`).
- **`silence_negative_prob=0.04` achieved 100.0% exact silence suppression (`0` FPs vs `35,462–39,959` FPs)** across `best_slot_f1.pt`, `best.pt`, and `last.pt` while pushing `32nd = 0.0559` (`5` TP) and `body = 0.8518`; optimal threshold shifted slightly to `0.55` (`n_pred = 40,221`), indicating `silence_negative_prob = 0.02` (or `pw = 1.15–1.20`) is cleaner for `1,713t`.
- **`d_model=384` improved `intro` (`0.8015 → 0.8200`, `+1.85 pp`) and `last.pt` (`0.83974`)**, but over-predicted (`n_pred = 40,468`, τ = `0.55`) and dropped `zeros` grounding to `−0.08191` (under our `0.10` guardrail), so **`d_model=256` stays the recommended `1,713t` backbone**.

| **Hypothesis** | In `EXP-20261128-01` (`1,713t → FT`), restricting soft-Dice to `16th_off + 24th` slots (`dice_slot_scope="fine_subdiv"`, `0.03`) cured sparse `<=8` over-prediction while lifting fine subdivisions for a `+0.47 pp` win (`0.79043`). Applying the same `fine_subdiv 0.03` loss from scratch on Dataset A (`81` songs) alongside `rdrop_kl_weight=2.0` — both at matched `bce_pos_weight=1.35` (`Arm A`) and calibrated `pw=1.00` (`Arm B`) — should cut sparse-chart errors and test whether `fine_subdiv 0.03` improves the from-scratch `EXP-20261127-02` (`best.pt 0.77831` / `best_slot_f1.pt 0.77757`) & `EXP-20261127-01` (`0.77971` peak / `0.77585 ± 0.00341` mean) literature benchmarks. |
| **Result** | **Partial / Tradeoff — new from-scratch calibration, error-count, and `shuffle` grounding records (`Arm B`), slightly trailing global Dice `EXP-20261127-02` on `M-slot48` (`45` val charts, `17,868` GT steps, fp32 eval):**<br>• **Baseline `EXP-20261127-02` (`global dice=0.05, pw=1.35, rdrop2, s=42`):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 16) `0.77757 @ 0.50` exact (`evF1 0.79643`, `8,134` err, `shuffle −0.1333`, `zeros −0.64991`); `best.pt` (ep 17) **`0.77831 @ 0.50`**; `last.pt` (ep 64) `0.76590`. (`EXP-20261127-01` peak no-rdrop `0.77971` / `0.80085` `evF1` / `8,024` err; 3-run mean `0.77585 ± 0.00341`.)<br>• **Arm A (`L4`, `fine_subdiv 0.03, pw=1.35, rdrop2, s=42`, `14.6 s/ep`):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 24): **`0.77621` F1@0.5** (`trainer 0.77625`, `f1_max 0.77621 @ 0.50` exact, **`evF1 = 0.79549`**, `8,219` err `TP=14,254 FP=4,605 FN=3,614`, `n_pred=18,859`, `r=1.055`, `P=0.7558 R=0.7977`), grounding **`shuffle −0.1433`** (`ev −0.1521`, `+1.00 pp` stronger than `EXP-20261127-02`), **`zeros −0.5057`** (`n_pred=3,899`). Slices: `quarter 0.8454` (`P=0.781 R=0.921`), `8th 0.7810`, **`16th_off 0.6470`**, **`24th 0.3375`** (`+1.45 pp` vs `rdrop2`), `<=8 0.7287` (`r=1.075`), `9–10 0.7847`, `11–12 0.8215`, `13+ 0.8527`, `intro 0.6350`, `body 0.7855`, `outro 0.7652`, `arrow_arrangements 0.8346`, `beast_beats 0.8208`, `tsunamix_iii 0.7298`. `best.pt` (ep 20, `val_loss 0.08685`): `0.77493` (`evF1 0.79524`, `8,336` err, `shuffle −0.1419`, **all-time Dataset A silence suppression `zeros −0.6525`**, `n_pred=1,497`, `24th 0.3581`). **`last.pt` (ep 64, `val_loss 0.09990`): `0.76807 @ 0.50` exact (`+0.217 pp` vs `EXP-20261127-02` `0.76590`)!** Worst charts (`best_slot_f1`): `west m=1 (0.278)`, `isis m=1/3/8/9/5 (0.282–0.439)`, `right_osiris m=1 (0.397)`.<br>• **Arm B (`T4`, `fine_subdiv 0.03, pw=1.00, rdrop2, s=42`, `21.3 s/ep`):** `best.pt` (ep 15, **`val_loss 0.07427`**): **`0.77655` F1@0.5** (`f1_max 0.77668 @ 0.45`), **`evF1 = 0.79664`** (beats `EXP-20261127-02` `0.79643`), **ALL-TIME FROM-SCRATCH LOWEST `7,984` errors (`TP=13,873 FP=3,989 FN=3,995`, first from-scratch Dataset A checkpoint `<8,000` errors!)**, **`17,862 / 17,868 = 99.97%` EXACT DENSITY (`r=0.9997`, `P=0.7767 R=0.7764`)**, **ALL-TIME FROM-SCRATCH `shuffle −0.1484` (`ev −0.1584`, beats `4/0.15` SpecAug `−0.1469`)**, `zeros −0.2719` (`n_pred=9,433`). Slices (`best.pt`): **`quarter 0.8512 (+0.58 pp vs Arm A)`**, `8th 0.7724`, `16th_off 0.6211`, `24th 0.3302`, **`<=8 0.7298` (`r=1.018` vs `1.075`)**, `9–10 0.7820`, `11–12 0.8212`, **`13+ 0.8562`**, **`intro 0.6601 (+2.51 pp vs Arm A)`**, `body 0.7849`, `outro 0.7587`, `arrow_arrangements 0.8285`, **`beast_beats 0.8230`**, **`tsunamix_iii 0.7309` (`−404` fewer FPs vs Arm A)**. `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 16): `0.77399` (`max 0.77460 @ 0.45`, `evF1 0.79400`, `8,153` err, `shuffle −0.1459`, `zeros −0.2342`, `24th 0.3511`). `last.pt` (ep 64, `val_loss 0.08738`): `0.76467` (`0.76633 @ 0.40`). |
| **Interpretation** | Unlike `1,713t → FT` (`EXP-20261128-01`, where `fine_subdiv 0.03` resolved inherited `pw=1.25` low-meter over-prediction for a `+0.47 pp` jump to `0.79043`), **from-scratch `81`-song Dataset A** already trained stably with global `dice=0.05 + pw=1.35` (`EXP-20261127-01/02`). Keeping `pw=1.35` without global quarter/8th Dice (`Arm A`) leaves excess quarter FPs (`r=1.055`, `quarter P=0.781`), whereas dropping `pw → 1.00` (`Arm B`) eliminates the over-prediction completely (**all-time from-scratch lowest `7,984` errors, `99.97%` density, strongest `shuffle −0.1484`, `+2.51 pp intro`, `evF1 0.79664`**) while trimming `16th_off` recall (`0.647 → 0.621`). Global Dice `EXP-20261127-01` (`0.77971` / `0.77585 ± 0.00341`) and `EXP-20261127-02` (`0.77831`) remain the top from-scratch Dataset A literature baselines (`pw=1.15` + `fine_subdiv` left optional if revisiting from-scratch tuning). |

| **Hypothesis** | (1) Averaging logits or weights of complementary fine-tuned models (`keep0 SpecAug` Arm C + `rdrop=2` Arm D + `ZS 1,713t`) may lift Dataset A without retraining; (2) Auditing the `12.2 pp` gap between `arrow_arrangements` (`0.856`) and `tsunamix_iii` (`0.734`) will clarify whether remaining errors are model blind spots or chart-grid artifacts; (3) Measuring grid-independent ±20 ms and ±50 ms Hungarian `event_f1` under automatic beat trackers (`librosa`, `PLP`, `beat_this` ISMIR-24) quantifies real-world end-to-end performance when no `.sm` beat grid exists. |
| **Result** | **Supported — 3 HIGH-IMPACT FINDINGS:**<br>• **Part 1 — Ensembles & Model Weight Soups (Dataset A `45` val charts):**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– *Same-basin fine-tuned combinations (`Arm C` + `Arm D`, both initialized from `1,713t` Ep 8):* `prob_ens_CD` (`0.78509` / `0.80781` `evF1` / `7,946` err), `logit_ens_CD` (`0.78509` / `0.78556` max @ `0.55`), `soup_CD` single model (`0.78508` / `0.80780` `evF1`, `shuffle −0.1379`, `zeros −0.1420`), `soup_CD_all4` (`0.78487`), `wiseft_75CD_25ZS` (`0.78386`). High correlation within the same pretrained basin means single **`Arm C` (`0.78574` @ `0.50` / `0.80834` `evF1`)** remains the best single-pass model.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– **Diverse 3-model ensemble `logit_ens_CD_plus_ZS` (`Arm C + Arm D + ZS 1,713t`):** Sets **two new all-time Dataset A highs** — **`F1_max = 0.78633 @ 0.55`** (`F1@0.5 = 0.78569`) and **`±20 ms evF1 = 0.80918`** (`7,971` err, `ratio=1.081`), combining `ZS`'s sharper `16th`/`24th` features with fine-tuned `C/D`'s `intro` & Fraxtil calibration.<br>• **Part 2 — Per-Song Error & `tsunamix_iii/isis` Root-Cause Audit (`Arm C`):**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– **8/9 validation songs score `0.764–0.868` F1** (`deal_with_it 0.8684`, `spirit 0.8526`, `kokeshi_neko_medley 0.8406`, `goin_under 0.8403`, `trap 0.8310`, `stay_awake 0.7840`, `right_osiris 0.7774`, `west 0.7641`) → **pooled 8/9-song F1 = `0.81928` and ±20 ms `evF1 = 0.81946`** (`0.02 pp` gap!).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– **ONE song (`tsunamix_iii/isis`) drives `1,701` errors (`21.4%` of ALL errors across 45 charts!) and drags Dataset A `M-slot48` down by `−3.35 pp` (`0.81928 → 0.78574`):** `isis` contains **31 rapid-fire BPM gimmicks** (`140 → 70.333 → 140 → 210` at **fractional beat `83.3333...`**), pushing **`61.4%` of its GT steps onto off-grid `micro`/`32nd` slots** (top slots: `0: 334`, `47: 303`, `42: 122`, `31: 119`, `21: 104`, vs `0.0–3.1%` on the other 8 songs). Crucially, on `isis`, exact `M-slot48` is `0.3274` while **grid-free ±20 ms `evF1` DOUBLES to `0.6564` (`+32.9 pp`!)**, proving the model is tracking the real musical beats while the `.sm` fractional-BPM gimmicks scramble slot indices (and accounting for nearly the entire `2.26 pp` Dataset A gap between `M-slot48 0.7857` and `evF1 0.8083`!). (Official Dataset A always retains all 45 charts / 9 songs including `isis`.)<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– Excluding `meter=1` beginner charts (`36/45` charts): `Arm C` scores `0.79832` `M-slot48` / `0.82023` `evF1`.<br>• **Part 3 — Real-World Raw-Audio Beat Tracking Robustness (`Arm C`, all `45` Dataset A val charts, grid-independent Hungarian `evF1` vs true `.sm` onset timestamps):**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `oracle` (`.sm` beat grid): beat F1@70 ms `1.0000`, med err `0.0 ms` → **`evF1@20ms 0.80834`** (`0.81946` no-isis) / **`evF1@50ms 0.81378`** (`ratio 1.076`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `oracle_const_bpm` (1 global median BPM + beat 0): beat F1 `0.9264`, med err `0.0 ms` → **`evF1@20ms 0.79604`** (`−1.23 pp`) / **`evF1@50ms 0.80386`** (`ratio 1.017`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `oracle_jitter_10ms` (`medErr 6.5 ms`): `evF1@20ms 0.77365` / **`evF1@50ms 0.81268`** (`−0.11 pp`!).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `oracle_jitter_20ms` (`medErr 13.5 ms`): `evF1@20ms 0.59435` / `evF1@50ms 0.76258`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `librosa.beat.beat_track` (DSP, raw audio): beat F1 `0.5625`, `medErr 47.3 ms` (halves `4/9` fast 175–225 BPM EDM songs) → **`evF1@20ms 0.03689`** (collapses!) / `evF1@50ms 0.46949`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `librosa.beat.plp` (DSP, raw audio): beat F1 `0.6388`, `medErr 60.4 ms` → `evF1@20ms 0.20529` / `evF1@50ms 0.43504`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– **`beat_this` (ISMIR-2024 neural beat tracker, raw audio, ZERO `.sm` metadata):** beat F1 **`0.7915`**, `medErr 12.9 ms` (`0.991` on `deal_with_it`, `0.998` on `spirit`) → **`evF1@50ms 0.77471` (`95.2%` of oracle `0.81378`, only `−3.91 pp` drop!)** / `evF1@20ms 0.58035` (`0.60255` no-isis, `ratio 1.054`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– **`beat_this_octave_matched`:** beat F1 **`0.8362`**, `medErr 12.8 ms` → **`evF1@50ms 0.77658`** (`95.4%` of oracle!) / **`evF1@20ms 0.61548`** (`0.63107` no-isis, `ratio 1.019`). |
| **Interpretation** | (1) Single-pass **`Arm C` (`0.78574` / `0.80834` `evF1`)** is already near the ensemble ceiling (`0.78633` / `0.80918` `evF1` with `CD+ZS`), confirming fine-tuned stability. (2) Nearly the entire `M-slot48` vs `evF1` gap (`2.26 pp`) and a `3.35 pp` headline drag on Dataset A comes from one gimmick-chart (`tsunamix_iii/isis`) with 31 fractional-beat BPM changes; on the 8 standard songs our model already achieves **`0.819` exact slot F1**. (3) Classic DSP beat tracking (`librosa`) is unusable for beat-slot stepchart generation (`0.037` @ ±20 ms), whereas a modern neural beat tracker (**`beat_this`**) makes end-to-end raw-audio generation genuinely viable today — retaining **`95.2%` of oracle performance at ±50 ms (`0.7747` vs `0.8138`)** with zero `.sm` beat grid, and showing that the remaining ±20 ms gap (`0.580 → 0.808`) matches `oracle_jitter_20ms` (`0.594` at `13.5 ms` med err), pointing directly to a simple song-level audio-transient offset snap to close the remaining ~12.9 ms beat-annotation phase bias. |

`best.pt` hits `0.4666` `+10.0 pp`!)**, **`intro 0.6883`** (`body 0.7877`, `outro 0.7481`), meters: **`<=8 0.7225` (`+6.43 pp` vs `300t` ZS `0.6582`, matching in-domain `0.7241`!)**, `9–10 0.7908`, `11–12 0.8357`, `13+ 0.8648`, packs: **`arrow_arrangements 0.8469`**, **`beast_beats 0.8381`**, `tsunamix_iii 0.7229`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `best.pt` (Ep 11) ZS: `0.77345` F1@0.5 / `0.79918` `evF1` (`8,647` err, `ratio=1.136`, **`16th_off 0.6891`**, **`24th 0.4666`**).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– *Zero-shot decoding (`--diagnose_decoding`, `best_slot_f1.pt`):* Because full `final_data` shrank the zero-shot density mismatch (`ratio 1.19 → 1.114`), **`fixed_05` (`0.77870` / `evF1 0.80337`) now beats `topk_density` (`0.76942` / `evF1 0.79673`, though `topk_density` cuts total errors `8,360 → 8,240` and hits `13+ 0.8718`)** and `loso_subdiv` (`0.77297` / `0.79861`) even zero-shot! |
| **Interpretation** | Scaling `5.8×` in unique charts (`294 → 1,713`) with density conditioning + multi-layer MERT (`L2+6+10+16`) unlocked two major scientific findings: (1) fine rhythmic subdivisions (`16th_off +3.57 pp → 0.7635`, `24th +5.64 pp → 0.5922`) and `intro` (`+3.12 pp → 0.8327`) were severely data-starved at `300t` and surge cleanly with song diversity while cutting `48v` errors by `9.4%` (`−1,086`) and boosting `shuffle` grounding to an all-time high (`−0.1371`); (2) full `final_data` finally supplies sufficient low-density (`<0.7` steps/beat) charts so that a single **zero-shot** model trained with **0 Fraxtil songs** now outperforms our best 3-run in-domain Fraxtil MERT mean (`0.77870` vs `0.77585`) and sets the all-time Dataset A ±20 ms `evF1` (`0.80337`) and `F1_max` (`0.78215`) records. Because `1,713` steps/ep peaked at **Ep 8 (`13.7k` steps)** and `val_loss` peaked at **Ep 11 (`18.8k` steps)** before gradual late overfitting through Ep 32, future full-scale runs should tighten to **`Cosine-16`** (and/or test `rdrop_kl_weight=1.0–2.0`), while **fine-tuning `1,713t` `best_slot_f1.pt` on Dataset A** is an immediate high-ROI path past `0.79+`. |

| **Interpretation** | On both 81-song and 294-song regimes, extra-parameter architectural heads consistently accelerate ep-16 memorization, whereas zero/tiny-parameter training objectives (`beat_aux_head`, masked soft-Dice, `bce_pos_weight`, R-Drop) deliver real, transferable gains. Note also a cache finding: `*.mert_l2_6_10_16.npy` is pre-averaged `(T, 1024)` (`n_mert_layers=1`), so per-layer mixing requires stacked caches (`*.mert_stack_*.npy`). |

### EXP-20261127-02: Bidirectional Bernoulli R-Drop (`rdrop_kl_weight=2.0 / 4.0`) — 2/2 matched-seed Dataset A win + all-time `last.pt` & `zeros` grounding records on both benchmarks

| Field | Value |
| ----- | ----- |
| **Timestamp** | 2026-11-27 05:22:00 |
| **Track** | `train` + `metric` (added default-off `rdrop_kl_weight: float = 0.0` in `ItgptRunConfig`, numerically-stable fp32 `compute_bernoulli_rdrop_kl` weighted by `valid_mask * grid_weights` in `src/stepcovnet/pytorch/itgpt/models.py`, and 2-pass training loop in `src/stepcovnet/pytorch/itgpt/trainers.py`; zero extra params, zero inference overhead) |
| **Config** | [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001_density_beataux010_dice005_pw135_rdrop2.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001_density_beataux010_dice005_pw135_rdrop2.json) (Dataset A RTX 2080 / T4) + `300t/50v` `keep0 + beataux015 + dice005 + pw125 + rdrop2` (`T4 --high-mem`) |
| **Hypothesis** | Keeping `dropout=0.1` (since `dropout=0.2` hurt) while enforcing bidirectional Bernoulli KL consistency between two stochastic dropout passes on each training batch closes the train/eval subnetwork gap, curbs ep-16→64 overfitting, and improves calibration & grounding. |
| **Result** | **Supported on Dataset A (PROMOTED — 2/2 matched-seed wins); sets all-time `last.pt` on `300t/50v`:**<br>• **Dataset A `rdrop_kl_weight=2.0` (`seed=42`, RTX 2080, `16.8 s/ep`):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 16) **0.77757** @ **`0.50` exact** (`+0.172 pp` vs `8f4b1cf` 3-run mean `0.77585`, `+0.300 pp` vs same-machine rerun `0.77457`; `evF1 0.79643`, `8,134` err); **`best.pt` (ep 17, `val_loss 0.09604`) `0.77831` @ `0.50` exact**; **`last.pt` (ep 64, `val_loss 0.11057`) `0.76590`** (+0.63 pp vs `8f4b1cf`); **`shuffle −0.1333`**, **all-time grounding record `zeros −0.64991`** (`zeros` F1 `0.1277`, `n_pred=1,606`).<br>• **Dataset A `rdrop_kl_weight=4.0` (`seed=42`, `eval_slot_f1_every=2`, RTX 2080):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 18) **0.77726** @ `0.50` exact (`8,153` err, **`16th_off 0.651`** all-time high, `intro 0.6582`, `zeros −0.5743`), **all-time Dataset A `last.pt 0.76840` @ `0.50` exact** (`+0.88 pp`, `val_loss 0.10865`), though `4.0` over-damped rare `24th` (`0.191` vs `0.323` at `2.0`).<br>• **Dataset A matched-pair `seed=123` (`rdrop=2.0`, Colab T4):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 16, `val_loss 0.08566`) **0.77446** (**`+0.120 pp`** vs matched `8f4b1cf` `seed=123` `0.77326`, **`−79` errs** `8,312` vs `8,391`, `16th_off 0.6453`, ep-64 `val_loss 0.10253` vs `0.11210`, **`zeros −0.23933`** `+5.17 pp` stronger). 2-seed mean (`rdrop=2.0`): `best_slot_f1 0.77602`, `best.pt 0.77639`, `last.pt 0.76132`, `zeros −0.4446`.<br>• **Stage-2 `300t/50v` `rdrop_kl_weight=2.0` (`keep0 + beataux015 + dice005 + pw125`, `T4 --high-mem`, `36.3 s/ep`):** **NEW ALL-TIME `300t/50v` `last.pt 0.84069` (`0.84244` max @ `0.40`, `+0.254 pp` vs `0.83815`, ep-64 `val_loss 0.143 → 0.11649`)**, **`intro 0.8053`**, `outro 0.8546`, `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 20) `0.84849` (`evF1 0.84854`, `11,679` err, `shuffle −0.1135`, `zeros −0.0898`, `16th_off 0.7253`, `24th 0.4838` damped `-5.20 pp`). |
| **Interpretation** | R-Drop cleanly tames late-epoch Transformer overfitting on both datasets with zero inference cost (`last.pt` `+0.63–0.88 pp` on Dataset A, `+0.25 pp` to `0.84069` on `300t/50v`). On `300t/50v`, `α=2.0` slightly over-damped rare `24th` triplets (`−5.20 pp`) compared to the `0.84974` peak champion (`EXP-20261127-01`); lighter `rdrop_kl_weight=0.5–1.0` is an attractive future knob when scaling to full `final_data`. |

| **Config** | Dataset A: [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001_density_beataux010_dice005_pw135.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001_density_beataux010_dice005_pw135.json) · `300t/50v`: [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_density_specaug_keep0_beataux015_dice005_pw125.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_density_specaug_keep0_beataux015_dice005_pw125.json) |

| **Result** | **Supported (PROMOTED — NEW ALL-TIME CHAMPIONS ON BOTH BENCHMARKS, with full factorization & bracketing):**<br>• **Stage 1 — Dataset A (`81/9`, prior champ `0.77286` / `0.79228` `evF1` / `8,166` err):**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– *Complete factorization proves non-trivial 3-way synergy:* `pw=1.35` solo crashes to `0.76669` (`−0.62 pp`, `8,637` err, `+7.18%` FP flood); `dice=0.25` solo `0.77198`; `dice=0.05` solo **`0.77423`** (`evF1 0.79682`, `16th 0.645`, `24th 0.371`); `beataux=0.15` solo `0.77252` @ `0.50` / **`0.77758` @ `0.40`** (`8,000` err, `intro 0.683`, `zeros −0.581`); `beataux=0.10 + dice=0.05` (`pw=1.0`) `0.77343` / `0.77563` @ `0.40` (**`7,992` err**); `beataux=0.03 + dice=0.05` `0.77297` / `0.77694` @ `0.40`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– **Combined Dataset A champion (`beataux=0.10 + dice=0.05 + pw=1.35`, RTX 2080):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 16) **`0.77971` F1@0.5** (`+0.69 pp`), **`0.77971` max @ `0.50` exact**, **FIRST `>0.800` ±20 ms `evF1` (`0.80085`)**, **`8,024` err**, `shuffle −0.142`, **`zeros −0.619`**, `16th_off 0.643`, `24th 0.366`, `intro 0.670`. Cleanly bracketed: `pw=1.25` (`0.77580`), `pw=1.50` (`0.77581`), `beataux=0.15` (`0.77667`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– *Multi-seed / cross-GPU calibration (3 runs of `beataux=0.10 + dice=0.05 + pw=1.35`):* `seed=42` RTX 2080 run 1 (`0.77971`), `seed=42` RTX 2080 run 2 (`0.77457`, `best.pt 0.77561`), `seed=123` Colab T4 (`0.77326`) → **3-run mean `0.77585 ± 0.00341` F1@0.5 / `0.79632 ± 0.00404` `evF1`** (`3/3` runs beat prior `0.77286` champ).<br>• **Stage 2 — `300t/50v` (`294/48`, prior `keep0` champ `0.84764` / `0.84808` max / `11,618` err / `last 0.83732`, all on Colab `T4 --high-mem`):**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– *Factorization & bracketing:* `beataux=0.10` solo `0.84704` (`zeros −0.1112`); `dice=0.05` solo **`0.84837`** (`0.84938` max, `11,595` err, `shuffle −0.1269`, `zeros −0.1090`); `beataux=0.03 + dice=0.05` `0.84737`; `dice=0.05 + pw=1.15` (no `beataux`) `0.84874` (`11,654` err, `last 0.83550`, `zeros −0.0882`); `beataux=0.10 + dice=0.05 + pw=1.10` `0.84700`; `beataux=0.10 + dice=0.05 + pw=1.25` **`0.84903`** (`evF1 0.84924`, `last 0.83802`, `zeros −0.0962`); `beataux=0.10 + dice=0.05 + pw=1.35` `0.84792` (`24th 0.5365`); `beataux=0.20 + dice=0.05 + pw=1.25` `0.84795` (`11,736` err, `zeros −0.0951`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– **ALL-TIME `300t/50v` CHAMPION (`keep0 + beataux=0.15 + dice=0.05 + pw=1.25`):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 20) **`0.84974` F1@0.5** (`+0.21 pp` vs `d625680` `0.84764`), **FIRST `0.85000` `F1_max`** (@ `0.45`), **`0.84987` ±20 ms `evF1`**, **all-time lowest `11,593` errors** (`TP 32,780 / FP 6,756 / FN 4,837`), **`best.pt 0.84783`** (`val_loss 0.08848`), **`last.pt 0.83815`** (`+0.08 pp` vs `0.83732`, `+0.40 pp` vs `dice005`), **`shuffle −0.1226`**, **`zeros −0.1018`** (cleanly passes both guardrails), `quarter 0.9399`, **`8th 0.8869`**, `16th_off 0.7278`, **`24th 0.5358`**, `mizuki 0.8530`, `meter_12 0.8545`. |
| **Interpretation** | The three components cancel each other's biases cleanly: `beat_aux_head` suppresses empty-beat FPs and strengthens silence grounding (`Δzero`) while nudging `τ_opt` down to `0.40`; `dice_loss_weight=0.05` directly optimizes F1 on hard `16th`/`24th` subdivisions; and `bce_pos_weight` (`1.35` on `ls=0.01` Dataset A, `1.25` on `ls=0.0` `300t/50v`) restores exact `τ ≈ 0.50` recall calibration. Promoted as the new locked PyTorch ITGPT-MERT recipe on both benchmarks. |

| **Track** | `post` + `metric` (added `decode_topk_slots` & `evaluate_decoding_strategies` in `src/stepcovnet/pytorch/itgpt/evaluation.py`, re-exported via `src/stepcovnet/itgpt/evaluation.py`, and `--diagnose_decoding` in `scripts/eval_itgpt_placement.py`; 5 strategies evaluated with strict leakage-free leave-one-song-out CV for per-subdivision thresholds: `fixed_05`, `topk_density`, `topk_clamped`, `loso_subdiv`, `loso_subdiv_topk`) |
| **Checkpoints** | In-domain (`4`): `300t/50v` `combo` (`EXP-20261126-15`), `keep0` (`EXP-20261126-16`), `keep0_nps` (`EXP-20261126-17`), Dataset A `density` (`EXP-20261126-15`) · Zero-shot (`3`): `300t keep0 → Dataset A`, `300t combo → Dataset A`, `Dataset A density → 300t/50v` |
| **Hypothesis** | Density-conditioned models already know the target total step budget `K = round(steps_per_beat * n_beats)` at inference time. Selecting each chart's top-`K` slots (`topk_density` / `topk_clamped`) or applying leave-one-song-out per-subdivision thresholds (`loso_subdiv` / `loso_subdiv_topk`) might beat `p >= 0.5` either in-domain (on rare 16th/24th slots) or under cross-dataset zero-shot density mismatch (`ratio != 1.0`). |
| **Result** | **Supported — clean, consistent decision rule across all 7 evaluations:**<br>• **In-domain (`4/4` runs, `ratio = 1.012–1.040`) — `fixed_05` wins every single run:**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `300t/50v combo`: `fixed_05` **0.84780** (`evF1 0.84796`, `11,655` err) > `loso_subdiv` `0.84568` (`24th 0.5504`, `16th 0.7323`, `ratio 1.099`) > `topk_density` `0.84539` (`11,632` err) > `loso_subdiv_topk` `0.84310`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `300t/50v keep0`: `fixed_05` **0.84764** (`evF1 0.84780`, `11,618` err) > `topk_density` `0.84526` > `loso_subdiv` `0.84266` > `loso_subdiv_topk` `0.84148`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `300t/50v keep0_nps`: `fixed_05` **0.84731** (`evF1 0.84749`, `11,715` err) > `topk_clamped` `0.84454` > `topk_density` `0.84430` > `loso_subdiv` `0.84222` (`24th 0.5312`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `Dataset A density`: `fixed_05` **0.77286** (`evF1 0.79228`, `8,166` err) > `loso_subdiv` `0.75859` (`24th 0.4387` `+10.6 pp`, `ratio 1.107`) > `topk_clamped` `0.75775` > `topk_density` `0.75649` (`24th 0.3837`).<br>• **Zero-shot `300t/50v → Dataset A` (over-predicting `ratio = 1.179–1.198`) — `topk_density` is a big free win:**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `keep0 → Fraxtil`: `fixed_05` `0.73286` (`evF1 0.75613`, `10,399` err, `ratio 1.179`) → **`topk_density` `0.73853` (`+0.57 pp` F1) / `evF1 0.76522` (`+0.91 pp`) / `9,344` err (`−1,055` fewer errors!)**, lifting `arrow_arrangements 0.7754 → 0.7904`, `beast_beats 0.8011 → 0.8080`, `<=8 0.6582 → 0.6648`, `13+ 0.8591 → 0.8615`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `combo → Fraxtil`: `fixed_05` `0.73307` (`evF1 0.75446`, `10,482` err, `ratio 1.198`) → **`topk_density` `0.73707` (`+0.40 pp` F1) / `evF1 0.76394` (`+0.95 pp`) / `9,396` err (`−1,086` fewer errors!)**, lifting `24th 0.2110 → 0.2510` and `13+ 0.8615 → 0.8648`.<br>• **Zero-shot `Dataset A (density) → 300t/50v` (under-predicting `ratio = 0.756`) — `loso_subdiv_topk` & `topk_clamped` win:**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `fixed_05` `0.69712` (`P=0.809 R=0.612`, `evF1 0.69715`, `ratio 0.756`) → **`topk_clamped` `0.69803` (`+0.09 pp`, `evF1 0.70316` `+0.60 pp`, `16th 0.5697 → 0.6135` `+4.38 pp`, `24th 0.1232 → 0.1712` `+4.80 pp`, `vocaloid 0.8095 → 0.8176`)** → **`loso_subdiv_topk` `0.70588` (`+0.88 pp` F1!) / `evF1 0.71186` (`+1.47 pp`!)**, lifting `mizuki 0.6255 → 0.6509` (`+2.54 pp`), `meter 9–10 0.6911 → 0.7263` (`+3.52 pp`), `13+ 0.6808 → 0.6933`. |
| **Interpretation** | Density-conditioned ITGPT-MERT is already well-calibrated in-domain (`ratio 1.01–1.04`), so hard top-`K` slightly hurts overall F1 (`−0.24` to `−1.64 pp`) by forcing extra picks on quiet/uncertain songs where probability mass is genuinely lower. Under zero-shot domain shift (`ratio 1.18–1.20` or `0.756`), however, enforcing the known target step budget via `topk_density` (zero calibration data needed) immediately recovers `+0.40–0.57 pp` F1 / `+0.91–0.95 pp` `evF1` and eliminates `>1,050` false-positive errors on Fraxtil, while `loso_subdiv_topk` adds `+0.88 pp` F1 / `+1.47 pp` `evF1` in the reverse direction. |

### EXP-20261126-17: Explicit 3rd `NPS` scalar condition token (`use_nps_condition=true`) is a wash on `300t/50v` (`0.84731`) and confirms 3-run stability of the `0.847+` tier

| Field | Value |
| ----- | ----- |
| **Timestamp** | 2026-11-26 19:35:00 |
| **Track** | `model` + `train` + `metric` (added `MIN_NPS = 0.0`, `MAX_NPS = 20.0` in `itgpt/constants.py`, `use_nps_condition: bool = False` in `ItgptModelConfig`, and a 3rd scalar condition token `nps = steps_per_beat * bpm / 60` before `level6` in `ItgptPlacementModel`, validated to require `difficulty_source == "density"`) |
| **Config** | [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_density_specaug_keep0_nps.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_density_specaug_keep0_nps.json) (`T4 --high-mem` `fp16`, `22.8 s/ep`, exact single-knob delta on top of `EXP-20261126-16` `keep0`) |
| **Hypothesis** | Stepchart physical intensity depends on notes per second (`NPS = steps_per_beat * bpm / 60`): `4.0` steps/beat at `120 BPM` (`8 NPS`) is moderate 16th streams, whereas `4.0` steps/beat at `200 BPM` (`13.3 NPS`) is extreme stamina streams. Providing `NPS` directly as a 3rd condition token `[density_tok, bpm_tok, nps_tok]` before `level6` might avoid requiring attention to synthesize a bilinear product. |
| **Result** | **Not supported (Wash / slightly behind `keep0` — keep `use_nps_condition=false`).** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 20, fp32 eval): `M-slot48` F1@0.5 **0.84731** (`−0.03 pp` vs `keep0` `0.84764`, `−0.05 pp` vs `combo` `0.84780`), `F1_max` **0.84743** @ `0.45`, ±20 ms `micro_event_f1` **0.84749**, `11,715` errors (`TP=32,504 FP=6,602 FN=5,113`, `+97` vs `keep0`'s `11,618`), `shuffle −0.1249`, `zeros −0.0888` (`n_pred=43,891`, weaker silence suppression than `keep0` `−0.1049`). `best.pt` (ep 14, `val_loss 0.06792`): `0.84479`; `last.pt` (ep 64): `0.83433` (`−0.30 pp` vs `keep0` `0.83732`). Slices: minor uptick on `16th_off` (`0.7274`) & `mizuki` (`0.8520`), offset by `8th` (`0.8817`), `vocaloid` (`0.8221`), and `13+` (`0.8682` vs `keep0` `0.8746`). |
| **Interpretation** | `level6` self-attention over `[density_tok, bpm_tok]` already captures the `density × bpm` interaction cleanly, and adding a redundant 3rd condition token slightly strengthens the audio-blind silence prior (`zeros` `n_pred 34,958 → 43,891`). Equally valuable: three independent runs (`combo` `0.84780`, `keep0` `0.84764`, `keep0_nps` `0.84731`) confirm the **`+0.85–0.90 pp` (`0.8388 → 0.8476+`) leap** over `#METER` is completely reproducible and not seed noise. **Keep `use_nps_condition=false`.** |

Peak trailed pure `density` (`0.77286`) because `16th_off` dipped `0.6353 → 0.5921` under stacked `ls=0.01 + SpecAugment`.<br>• **Dataset A `density + SpecAugment (4/0.15) + keep0` (`ls=0.01`, `L4` `bf16`, `13.0 s/ep`):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 20): `0.76606` (`evF1 0.78955`, `8,464` err, `shuffle −0.1391`, `zeros −0.3751`, `last 0.75470`). Completely restored `16th_off` (`0.5921 → 0.6349`, `+4.27 pp`) and `zeros` (`−0.2275 → −0.3751`), while pure `density` (`0.77286`, `ls=0.01`, no SpecAugment) stays Dataset A peak. |
| **Interpretation** | Preserving beat frame `0` is a clean, zero-cost upgrade on `final_data` (`ls=0.0`), delivering our lowest error count (`11,618`), strongest `shuffle`/`zeros` grounding, highest `best.pt` (`0.84558`), and highest `last.pt` (`0.83732`) while protecting `8th` & `intro` downbeat transients. On 81-song Dataset A where `ls=0.01` already regularizes strongly, stacking SpecAugment trades `~0.46 pp` peak F1 for record `24th` (`0.3776`) & `shuffle` (`−0.1469`). **Lock `density + SpecAugment (4/0.15) + preserve_downbeat=true` (`ls=0.0`) as the full `final_data` scaling recipe**, and keep pure `density` (`ls=0.01`, `SpecAug=0/0.0`) as the Dataset A champion. |

On Dataset A, `density` alone establishes a new all-time PyTorch + Keras record (`0.77286` / `0.79228` `evF1`) and proves scale-invariant conditioning helps even within a single community's 5-chart progression. |

### EXP-20261126-14: Target-density conditioning (`difficulty_source="density"`) breaks the `0.8388` `300t/50v` ceiling (**`0.84071`** F1@0.5 / **`0.84174`** max-F1, `−160` errors) and beats in-domain Fraxtil MERT zero-shot on `13+`, `16th`, and `24th`

| Field | Value |
| ----- | ----- |
| **Timestamp** | 2026-11-26 17:41:00 |
| **Track** | `model` + `train` + `metric` (added default-off `difficulty_source: "meter" \| "density"` in `ItgptDatasetConfig`, `datasets.pack_chart`, `ItgptPlacementModel`, `evaluation._difficulty_source`; `"density"` replaces `#METER` with scale-invariant scalar `steps_per_beat = total_steps / n_beats` scaled on `[0, 6]`, same 2 condition tokens, zero extra parameters, zero timing leakage) |
| **Config** | [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_density.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_density.json) (`T4 --high-mem` `fp16`, `22.4 s/ep`; zero-shot eval on Dataset A `L4` via `scripts/eval_itgpt_placement.py`, which auto-resolves `model.difficulty_source == "density"`) |
| **Hypothesis** | `EXP-20261126-10` showed `final_data` mixes DDR (`mizuki`) and ITG (`itl_online_2026` / `vocaloid`) meter scales, causing both cross-pack ambiguity and zero-shot calibration errors. Replacing `#METER` with scale-invariant chart mean `steps_per_beat` removes meter-scale noise without adding timing leakage, potentially breaking the `0.8388` `300t/50v` ceiling and closing the `300t/50v → Dataset A` zero-shot gap. |
| **Result** | **Supported — PROMOTED as NEW ALL-TIME `300t/50v` CHAMPION (`0.84071` F1@0.5 / `0.84174` max-F1).**<br>• **In-domain `300t/50v` (`48` frozen val charts, fp32 eval):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 12): `M-slot48` F1@0.5 **0.84071** (**`+0.19 pp`** over `EXP-20261126-07` `0.83883`, breaking the `0.8388` ceiling held across 5 previous `#METER` runs), `F1_max` **0.84174** @ `0.40` (**`+0.29 pp`**), ±20 ms `micro_event_f1` **0.84074**, **`12,116` total errors** (`TP=31,973 FP=6,472 FN=5,644`, **−160 fewer errors** than `12,276`), `shuffle −0.1185`, `zeros −0.0854` (`n_pred=36,744`). `best.pt` (ep 8) broke `val_loss < 0.070` for the first time (`0.06966` → `0.83700` / `0.83743` max). `last.pt` (ep 64): **0.82476** (`+0.39 pp` vs `0.82089`). **Slice breakdown vs `EXP-20261126-07` (`#METER`):** **`16th_off 0.6881 → 0.7042` (+1.61 pp)**, **`meter 13+ 0.8496 → 0.8645` (+1.49 pp)**, **`vocaloid 0.8135 → 0.8178` (+0.43 pp)**, **`mizuki 0.8419 → 0.8442` (+0.23 pp)**, `24th 0.4906 → 0.4945` (+0.39 pp), `body 0.8400 → 0.8440` (+0.40 pp), with minor dips on `intro` (`0.8067 → 0.7849`) and `<=8` (`0.7815 → 0.7766`).<br>• **Zero-shot `300t/50v (density) → Dataset A (Fraxtil 45 val charts)`:** `best.pt` (ep 8): **0.73620** F1@0.5 / **0.74144** max @ `0.60` / **0.75424** ±20 ms `event_f1` (`shuffle −0.1181`, `zeros −0.1750`, `n_pred/n_ref = 1.166`); `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 12): `0.73260` F1@0.5 / `0.73614` max / `0.75472` `event_f1` (`shuffle −0.1227`, `zeros −0.1629`, `ratio = 1.192`). **Slices (`best_slot_f1` zero-shot vs `EXP-20261126-10` `#METER` zero-shot & `EXP-20261126-06` in-domain Fraxtil MERT):** inside shared density support (`meter 9+`), density conditioning matches or **beats the in-domain Fraxtil MERT model zero-shot** — **`meter 11–12` `0.8152`** (`+1.41 pp` vs `#METER` ZS `0.8011`, within `0.19 pp` of in-domain `0.8171`), **`meter 13+` `0.8584`** (`+1.67 pp` over in-domain `0.8416`), **`16th_off` `0.6343`** (`+2.34 pp` over in-domain `0.6109`), **`24th` `0.2761`** (`+15.76 pp` over `#METER` ZS `0.1185` and **`+9.28 pp` over in-domain `0.1833`**!). On `best.pt` ZS, density ratio is calibrated on `13+` (`1.033`), `11–12` (`1.081`), `9–10` (`1.070`), while `<=8` remains inflated (`ratio 1.286`, `F1 0.6709`; `0.6584` on `best_f1`). |
| **Interpretation** | Replacing ambiguous `#METER` with scale-invariant `steps_per_beat` immediately breaks the `300t/50v` ceiling (`0.83883 → 0.84071`, `−160` errors) by resolving high-density 16th-stream ambiguity (`16th_off +1.61 pp`, `meter 13+ +1.49 pp`). Two honest caveats: (1) on in-domain silence (`zeros`), exact density conditioning weakens `zeros` drop (`−0.1706 → −0.0854`) because the scalar prior predicts a denser step template even without audio (`shuffle` stays strong at `−0.1185`); (2) zero-shot on Dataset A `<=8` stays inflated (`ratio 1.286`) because `300t/50v` has almost no Beginner/Easy (`<0.7` steps/beat) training charts — proving the remaining zero-shot gap is **low-density training support**, not meter-scale mismatch. Strongly complements `SpecAugment (4/0.15)` (`16th`/`13+` win vs `24th`/`shuffle`/`last` win). |

| **Configs** | [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_specaug_lite.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_specaug_lite.json) (`300t/50v`, `T4 --high-mem`) · [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001_specaug_lite.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001_specaug_lite.json) (Dataset A, `L4`) |

Slices: `24th` rose `0.491 → 0.521` (`+3.0 pp`, below `4/0.15`'s `0.535`), `8th` still dipped `−0.79 pp`, and `meter 13+` fell `−0.88 pp`.<br>• **Dataset A (`L4` `bf16`, `12.9 s/ep`):** `best.pt` (ep 27): **0.76389** F1@0.5 (`−0.53 pp` vs `EXP-20261126-06` `0.7691`, `evF1 0.77977`, `8,683` errors, `shuffle −0.1370`, `zeros −0.1264`); `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 36): `0.76225` (`−0.40 pp` vs `4/0.15` `0.76621`, `evF1 0.78025`, `shuffle −0.1403`); `last.pt`: `0.75346` (failed to prevent `ls=0.01` late drift, unlike `4/0.15`'s `0.76424`). Slices (`best.pt`): hit a new Dataset A `16th_off` peak (`0.6525`, **`+4.16 pp`**) and boosted `8th` (`+1.11 pp`), yet `quarter` **still dipped `−1.65 pp`** (`0.8477 → 0.8311`) and `<=8` dipped `−1.27 pp`. |
| **Interpretation** | Random sub-beat frame masking occasionally zeroes frame 0 (the quarter-note transient) even at `max_frames = 2`, so halving SpecAugment keeps the `quarter` penalty while losing `4/0.15`'s strong late-epoch regularization, `zeros`/`shuffle` grounding, and `24th` gains. **Keep default `0 / 0.0`** (or use `4 / 0.15` when targeting `24th` / `shuffle` grounding / `last` stability). |

### EXP-20261126-11: MERT-only SpecAugment sets all-time `shuffle` grounding & `24th` triplet records on both `300t/50v` and Dataset A while lifting late-epoch stability `+0.77/+1.34 pp`

| Field | Value |
| ----- | ----- |
| **Timestamp** | 2026-11-26 16:04:00 |
| **Track** | `model` + `train` + `metric` (MERT-only SpecAugment: `mert_time_mask_max_frames=4` + `mert_channel_dropout=0.15` inside `ItgptBeatMertEncoder` during training only, evaluated on both `300t/50v` `T4 --high-mem` and Dataset A `L4`) |
| **Configs** | [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_specaug.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_ladder_300t_50v_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_specaug.json) (`300t/50v`) · [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001_specaug.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001_specaug.json) (Dataset A) |
| **Hypothesis** | Unlike global `dropout=0.2` (which degraded both peak F1 and `zeros` grounding by dropping metadata/grid pathways), applying SpecAugment masking **only** to MERT input features (up to `4/24` contiguous frames + `15%` channel dropout per beat during training) will curb memorization, lift late-epoch stability, and improve audio grounding and fine subdivisions without hurting peak capacity. |
| **Result** | **Partial / Strong Grounding, Stability & Fine-Subdivision Win across BOTH benchmarks.**<br>• **`300t/50v` (`T4 --high-mem` `fp16`, `22.0 s/ep`):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 28, fp32 eval): `M-slot48` F1@0.5 **0.83855** (`max-F1 0.83855` @ `0.50` exact calibration, **`+0.59 pp`** vs baseline `best_slot_f1` `0.83268`, within `0.028 pp` of baseline `best.pt` `0.83883`), ±20 ms `micro_event_f1` **0.83863**, `12,389` errors (`FP=6,946 FN=5,443`), **new all-time `300t/50v` `shuffle` grounding record (`−0.1249`)**, and full `zeros` suppression (`−0.1703`). `last.pt` (ep 64): **0.82863** (**`+0.77 pp`** over baseline `0.82089`, cutting ep-64 `val_loss` `0.1811 → 0.1175`). **Subdivision breakdown (`best_slot_f1` vs `EXP-20261126-07` baseline):** **`24th` jumped `0.491 → 0.535` (+4.4 pp vs baseline, +3.1 pp NEW ALL-TIME RECORD over Keras L6 `0.504`)**, `16th_off` rose `0.688 → 0.697`, `other` (32nd/48th) became non-zero (`0.066`, `P=0.67`) for the first time, and `itl_online_2026` hit `0.856`.<br>• **Dataset A (`L4` `bf16`, `14.3 s/ep`):** `best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 48, fp32 eval): `M-slot48` F1@0.5 **0.76621** (`−0.29 pp` vs `EXP-20261126-06` `0.7691`; `max-F1 0.76986` @ `0.40`; `8,323` errors), ±20 ms `event_f1` **0.77970**, **new all-time Dataset A `shuffle` grounding record (`−0.1468`, `8.1×` mel DDCL)**, **`zeros −0.5328`** (`4.4×` stronger than `EXP-20261126-06`'s `−0.1220`). `last.pt` (ep 64): **0.76424** (**`+1.34 pp`** over `EXP-20261126-06` `0.7508`, completely eliminating `ls=0.01`'s late drift; `val_loss` stayed flat `0.0626–0.0646` across ep 12–64). **Slices (`best.pt` vs `EXP-20261126-06`):** **`16th_off 0.611 → 0.647` (+3.6 pp)**, **`24th 0.183 → 0.221` (+3.8 pp)**, `meter 13+ 0.842 → 0.849` (+0.7 pp), `intro 0.629 → 0.633`, while `quarter` dipped `0.848 → 0.833`. |
| **Interpretation** | Masking sub-beat MERT frames/channels forces ITGPT to rely on broader rhythmic context rather than memorizing single-frame MERT spikes, yielding consistent `+3–4 pp` fine-subdivision (`16th`/`24th`) gains, all-time `shuffle` grounding records on both datasets (`−0.1249` / `−0.1468`), and `+0.77` to `+1.34 pp` late-epoch stability. Default remains `0 / 0.0` (peak F1@0.5 slightly trails unmasked by `0.03–0.29 pp`), making mild SpecAugment (e.g. `max_frames=2, ch_drop=0.10`) a strong optional regularizer to revisit at full `final_data` scale if needed. |

### EXP-20261126-04: PyTorch ITGPT-MERT Dataset A parity run (`Colab L4` `bf16`) runs 2.8× faster and matches ±20 ms `event_f1` (0.7814) & grounding, revealing and fixing a non-deterministic `val_iter` bug

| Field | Value |
| ----- | ----- |
| **Timestamp** | 2026-11-26 09:18:00 |
| **Track** | `train` + `metric` (PyTorch cutover parity check on Dataset A `L2+6+10+16 + Cosine-64 + EMA` vs Keras champion `EXP-20261125-10`) |
| **Config** | [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema.json) |
| **Hypothesis** | The native PyTorch ITGPT implementation (`src/stepcovnet/pytorch/itgpt/`) matches Keras `EXP-20261125-10` (**0.7680** F1@0.5 / **0.7698** max-F1 / **0.7814** ±20 ms `micro_event_f1` / `last` **0.7650**) on Dataset A while improving GPU throughput via `bf16` + SDPA FlashAttention. |
| **Result** | **Partial parity + concrete trainer bug found & fixed (`2.8×` faster at `17.3 s/ep` vs Keras `47 s/ep`).** **`best_slot_f1.pt` (ep 24, fp32 eval):** `M-slot48` F1@0.5 **0.7623** (`−0.57 pp` vs Keras `0.7680`, `TP=13557 FP=4143 FN=4311`, `8,454` errors vs `8,182`, `n_pred 17,700` vs `17,868`), **`F1_max 0.7675` @ `0.40`** (`−0.23 pp` vs `0.7698`), **±20 ms `micro_event_f1 0.7814`** (exact match to Keras `0.7814`), stronger audio grounding (`shuffle −0.1407` vs `−0.1396`, event drop `−0.1498`; `zeros −0.2408` vs `−0.2308`, event drop `−0.2496`, `1 − IoU 0.561`). **`best.pt` (ep 18, `val_loss 0.03216`):** **0.7618** F1@0.5 / **0.7655** max-F1 (`TP=13559 FP=4170 FN=4309`). **`last.pt` (ep 64, `train_loss 0.01683`, `val_loss 0.05938`):** **0.7517** F1@0.5 (`−1.33 pp` vs Keras `0.7650`) / **0.7546** max-F1 (`−1.63 pp` vs `0.7709`). |
| **Interpretation** | Line-by-line audit of `src/stepcovnet/pytorch/itgpt/{models,trainers,datasets}.py` and `runtime.py` against pre-cutover Keras (`946cc8f`) confirmed 1:1 layer/init/dropout/loss/EMA parity and isolated **one real trainer bug** plus two documented framework differences: **(1) Bug (fixed):** `val_iter` (ITGPT) and `val_rng` (DDCL/DDC) were instantiated once outside the epoch loop instead of resetting to `seed + 1` each epoch like Keras `model.fit(validation_data=...)`, so each epoch scored a different random 20-chart sample (`val_loss` bounced `±0.020`, e.g. `0.040 → 0.032 → 0.048`, making `best.pt` pick a lucky ep-18 draw instead of the true optimum; Keras's `0.7680` came from smooth-`val_loss` `best.keras` at ep 41). Fixed across `itgpt`, `ddcl`, and `ddc` with a regression test. **(2) Gradient clipping:** Keras `AdamW(clipnorm=1.0)` clips per-tensor L2 norms while PyTorch `clip_grad_norm_(..., 1.0)` clips global L2 norm (matching upstream ITGPT). **(3) Precision/kernels:** PyTorch L4 runs `bf16` + SDPA (`2.8×` faster) vs Keras `fp16` + `LossScaleOptimizer`, producing faster convergence and earlier late-epoch memorization on the 81-song train set. Ready for optional Dataset A L4 re-check (~18 min) and `300t/50v` stacked transfer verification. |

| **Config** | [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001.json) · [`configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls002.json`](../../configs/ddc/itgpt_mert_placement_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls002.json) |

| **Result** | **Supported on `best.keras` & grounding (NEW DATASET A RECORDS across `slot_f1_at_05`, `slot_f1_max`, `micro_event_f1`, `skill_event_f1`, `shuffle`, and `zeros`), with late-epoch `last` drift.** **`ls = 0.01` (Colab `L4`, winner):** `best.keras` (ep 26, `val_loss 0.06006`, fp32) hit **`M-slot48` F1@0.5 0.7711** (`+0.31 pp` vs `0.7680`, `TP=13893 FP=4275 FN=3975`, `8,250` errors, `n_pred 18,168` vs `17,868`), **`F1_max 0.7722` @ `0.45`** (`+0.24 pp`), **±20 ms `micro_event_f1 0.7873`** (`+0.59 pp`), **`skill_event_f1 +0.6904`** (`+0.91 pp`), and stronger audio grounding (`shuffle −0.1427` / event `−0.2208`; `zeros −0.7357` / event `−0.6904`, `skill → 0.0`). `best_slot_f1.keras` (ep 16): **0.7706 @ `0.50`**. `last.keras` (ep 64): `0.7556` (`max 0.7581 @ 0.35`, `−0.94 pp` vs baseline `0.7650`). **`ls = 0.02` (Colab `T4`):** `best.keras` (ep 17, `val_loss 0.07344`, fp32) hit **`M-slot48` F1@0.5 0.7711** (`0.77115` @ **`0.50`** exact, `TP=14048 FP=4518 FN=3820`, `8,338` errors, `n_pred 18,566`), `micro_event_f1 0.7860`, `skill_event_f1 +0.6904`, `shuffle −0.1353`, `zeros −0.7295`; `best_slot_f1.keras` (ep 16) **0.7703 @ `0.50`**; `last.keras` (ep 64) `0.7574` (`max 0.7599`). |
| **Interpretation** | Mild label smoothing (`0.01`) is the only loss/regularization knob to beat plain grid-weighted BCE on Dataset A, breaking `0.770` at `@0.50` (`0.7711` / `0.7722` max / `+0.6904` skill) while strengthening both `shuffle` and `zeros` audio grounding (`0.0` skill on silence). Because smoothed targets shift late-epoch memorization after ep ~26 (`last` `0.7556` vs unsmoothed `0.7650`), keep `label_smoothing = 0.0` as default and test `0.01` vs `0.0` directly on `300t/50v` where 3.6× more charts reduce overfitting. |
| **Artifacts** | `MyDrive/StepCOVNet/runs/itgpt_mert_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls001/{best,best_slot_f1}.keras`, `MyDrive/StepCOVNet/runs/itgpt_mert_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls002/{best,best_slot_f1}.keras`, `experiments/colab/itgpt_mert_fraxtil_orig_cosine64_l2_6_10_16_ema_ls00{1,2}/eval_val_grounding_best.json` |

Audio grounding: `shuffle` drop **−0.1304**, `zeros` drop **−0.2955**. | **Interpretation** | ITGPT's hierarchical 16/32/64-beat chunks (`level3..5`) and `level6_pos(0..32)` are musically anchored to song start (`beat 0`); shifting 94% of training charts away from beat 0 starved beat-0 intro representations and misaligned 4-bar/8-bar/16-bar musical phrases between train and unshifted eval. **Keep `max_bar_shift = 0`.** |

| **Interpretation** | Raw STFT flux/energy spikes carry mastering- and timbre-dependent variance across songs that generalizes worse than self-supervised MERT features — echoing the log-mel overfitting gap (`EXP-20261119-03`). **Keep `mert_spectral_flux = false`**; pure multi-layer MERT (`layers 2,6,10,16`) needs no `*.ddc_mel.npy` extraction when scaling `final_data`. |

Audio grounding stays strong: `shuffle` drop **−0.1419** (**7.8×** mel DDCL), `zeros` drop **−0.1500**. Error breakdown: `intro (0–10%)` jumped **+3.6 pp** (`0.626 → 0.663`), `outro` **0.761**, `8th` **+2.2 pp** (`0.740 → 0.762`), `fraxtil_s_arrow_arrangements` **0.8413** (`+1.33 pp`), `tsunamix_iii` **0.7166** (best across all runs); rare `24th` triplet spikes (`548` GT slots) smoothed down (`0.253 → 0.078`) on the small 81-song train set. |
| **Interpretation** | Unlike `gradaccum4` (which cut update count 4× and under-trained), EMA preserves all `12,800` AdamW steps while continuously smoothing `batch_size=1` weight noise — matching our free checkpoint-soup hypothesis with zero extra inference cost or RAM. **Promote `use_ema = true` (`ema_momentum = 0.995`)** alongside Cosine-64 and multi-layer MERT. |

### EXP-20261125-08: `gradient_accumulation_steps = 4` under-trains at fixed 64-ep `lr=1e-4` budget (0.7491 vs 0.7664) — rejected
